Fol. 53.

In a glass vessel, excellently sealed with clay, place it upon the furnace and begin to distill the <term>Water</term> <note>symbol: 🜄</note>. Continue driving it out as long as you wish to proceed, but take 7 parts of the <term>Aqua Vitae</term> <note>literally "Water of Life"; original: "∇ vitæ"; usually refers to highly distilled alcohol or a potent solvent</note> and put it into two well-sealed <term>cucurbits</term> <note>gourd-shaped distilling vessels</note> and keep it. Note that you should drive it with a strong <term>Fire</term> <note>symbol: 🜂</note>. Then take that which remained at the bottom, grind it finely, and put it into a small glass; then take the "wine" from these glasses with the <term>Aqua Vitae</term> and pour it over this <term>Salt</term> <note>symbol: □; likely the "Salt of Antimony" mentioned on the previous page</note> in another glass. Close it with the <term>Seal of Hermes</term> <note>an airtight glass seal made by melting the neck of the vessel</note>. Let them be placed in the furnace of digestion with a slow <term>Fire</term> <note>symbol: 🜂</note>; thus the <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note> of <term>Antimony</term> <note>symbol: δ; here likely referring to the metallic body</note> will be dissolved again into a white <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note>. This will happen in the space of 11 or 12 weeks. Take this <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note> and project it upon the <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note> of ash in the <term>Fire</term> <note>symbol: 🜂</note>, and it will begin to smoke and become the final <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note>.

If indeed you wish to proceed from the white to the red, again divide the <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note> into 7 parts. Include 1 part of this <term>Silver</term> <note>symbol: ☾</note> powder in a small glass and pour over it another part of the <term>Aqua Vitae</term> which you previously reserved in the other glass. Place it in the digestion furnace and it will dissolve. In 24 <term>hours</term> <note>symbol: ☉; the sun symbol here denotes a full day/night cycle</note> leave it to dissolve and the <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note> [turns] into the red <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note>. That Red substance will <term>tinge</term> <note>alchemical term for transforming or dyeing the nature of a metal</note> raw <term>medicine</term> <note>symbol: ℞</note> into true <term>Gold</term> <note>symbol: ☉</note>. 1 part [to] 100 parts.

# The Summary of the Philosopher’s Stone
## A SIX-LINE POEM.

> Pour wine for the Old Man, until by urinating the liquid
> He has returned it; this way alone lies open to the Stone of the Wise.
> Then a lake will be made in which you must submerge chaste Diana,
> Whose brother you shall then leave to sleep in her lap,
> So that both, wounded by a single blow, may perish together.
> Thus you shall achieve your desires, after they rise again from the fire.

->Explanation.<-

These verses contain the whole and true process of the Philosophical Work from the beginning to the very end. If anyone, after reading them, should later wish to attempt an erroneous path, they may do penance as far as I am concerned. For just as there is only one God, so there is only one truth, which can be reached only through prayers and sober conversation. Lest you be deceived—unless you wish to be deceived of your own accord—listen joyfully to the true exposition of the preceding verses. If by its benefit you come to the knowledge of the most precious secret, Praise God, and be charitable to your neighbor.

->The Old Man<-

Fol. 54

That old man to whom you must pour the wine is Saturn <note>original: ♄</note>. He is one of the higher planets, who communicates his virtues to the others and is capable of destroying them. <margin>* I think it is rather Saturn <note>original: ♄</note>.</margin>

<margin>Matter upon which one must work.</margin> Therefore, take Saturn <note>original: ♄</note> and convert it into a calx <note>a powder produced by heating a metal; an oxide</note>, * in the original: and make the arsenic of Saturn. For its inner parts are red. Therefore, Saturn is red lead, but beware of the common variety because of its mixtures; for this destroys everything, and nothing in this art succeeds well except that which is pure and simple.

Take 6 pounds of this calx of Saturn and soak it with wine, and purify it as much as you can. Therefore, if you wish to work toward the white stage, take Silver <note>original: ☽a</note> with an equal weight of saltpeter <term>sal petrae|potassium nitrate</term> through many turns, taking new salt each time. But if you work toward the white and the red stages, take Gold <note>original: ☉</note> with equal parts of Antimony <note>original: ♁j.</note> and the Mercury of Gold <note>original: ☿ ☉</note>.

Then add 2 quarts of spirit of wine <term>spiritus vini|distilled alcohol or ethanol</term> or of pure and unmixed wine, and store it in a cellar for many [days], or five or six, for digestion, stirring it often. Filter it, and with a slow fire <note>original: △</note> draw off the wine. Leave the remaining material for 20 days for putrefaction <note>the stage of the alchemical process where the matter turns black and "dies" before rebirth</note>, and with a stronger fire <margin>* 5. The Quintessence.</margin> you will see a golden liquor—the true Mercury, namely the Quintessence, for our work.

Then with a slow fire <note>original: △</note>, moisten it with the Quintessence; afterward, with a stronger fire, draw off the Spirit of Jupiter <note>original: ♃</note>, which is our pure Science, the Quintessence, and the Spirit of the Philosophers. <margin>Spirit of the Philosophers.</margin>

-> After which you will leave her brother to sleep in her lap, <-

Jupiter <note>original: ♃</note>. To this Spirit of Jupiter, apply as much Gold <note>original: ☉</note> as can be [dissolved <unclear>m m</unclear>]. And when it can no longer dissolve, <margin>The joining of the luminaries <note>the Sun and Moon</note>.</margin> take care not to submerge it; for it will not rejoice in a greater quantity than it is fit to contain, and too much ruins the whole Work because of the Element of Fire <note>original: △</note>. But you must also take care that the Gold is pure and perfect, otherwise you might fall into the greatest error.

Therefore, let the Gold be natural, and corrupted as little as possible by any preceding melting. <margin>Gold chosen for this work.</margin>

# Fol. 55.

This oil, administered by itself, performs wonders in all cold ailments <note>In Galenic medicine, diseases were classified by "humors"; "cold" ailments included paralysis or chills</note>. In apoplexy <note>a sudden loss of consciousness or a stroke</note> and all types of convulsions, half a spoonful in half a pint of Rhenish wine <note>original: "vini Abenani," likely a transcription error for "Rhenani," wine from the Rhine region</note> or another liquid is a most effective remedy. It is true fire <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note>. Therefore, you are advised to beware of using too great a quantity of the Sun <note>original symbol: $\odot$; here referring to the "Philosophical Sun" or intense gold-level heat</note> in the first dissolution, lest you turn your matter into powder and a premature birth <note>an alchemical metaphor for a failed or ruined process</note> results.

<margin>
The medical virtues of the aforementioned Sun <note>original: $\odot$</note> or Philosophical Fire <note>original: $\triangle$</note>.
</margin>

After you have imitated the Supreme Master of Nature in this manner in the **Division of the Elements**; your second care should be to join together again those things which you previously separated. Therefore, you must consider the nature of that final element, which is oily; it must necessarily affect the earth left behind at the bottom with a certain greasy touch. However, since that earth is as black as night, you must first take care to dry it and calcine <term>calcines|heating a substance to high temperatures to achieve a state of dry powder or oxide</term> it before it is capable of receiving those elements which you first drew away from it. Nothing, however, should be diminished or taken away from the matter,

# Fol. 56.

You must do this, otherwise you have labored in vain. The reason for this is that if anything were removed, an inequality would arise among the Elements. However, the equality and balance of the Elements is the very summit and the 
<margin>The Summit of the Great Work <note>original: "Apex Magisterij"</note></margin>
highest point of the Great Work.

After this, divide your element of Water <note>original symbol: $\nabla$</note> into seven parts, according to the number of the
<margin>The Mystery of the Seven <note>original: "Septenarij Mysteriu"</note></margin>
days of creation; and in all your operations, remember the number seven. Take the Spirit <note>original symbol: $\text{H}$, likely referring to the Mercurial spirit or "Hydrargyrum"</note> as the seventh part of the Quintessence <note>the "fifth essence," the purest part of a substance</note> and soak the earth—or rather, a small portion of it—and thus join a small portion to each part. Proceed by soaking it little by little, always drying the earth in the meantime, so that it may receive the Water <note>original symbol: $\nabla$</note> better and soak it up more readily.

Then you will see the earth, which before was as if dead, excited back to life and made a little brighter. Therefore, you must first take care that you do not run out of the Silver Spirit <note>original symbol: $\text{D}$, often representing the Moon/Silver or a specific white distillate</note> of the Quintessence, and that you have a greater supply prepared than what was prescribed at the beginning of this work; for indeed, it is of greater importance than can be conceived at first glance.

Then, divide the Fire <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note> similarly into seven parts and

<column-break/>

soak it in exactly the same way as you did the Water <note>original symbol: $\nabla$</note>, but without the addition of the Quintessence.

Then the earth will become white like snow; and it is called
<margin>Aloes and Feminine Fire <note>original: "Aloes & $\triangle$ famineu"</note></margin>
"Female Aloes," or "Fire of a feminine quality." This is because, as was told before, it consists of only three elements and lacks the generative power of fire. If you wish to add this power to perform the projection <term>projection|the final stage of alchemy where the "philosopher's stone" is cast upon base metals to transform them into gold or silver</term>, you must divide the matter equally, take one part, and place it in a vessel. At the same time, take one part of the Spirit <note>original symbol: $\text{H}$</note> and all of the Fire <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note>. Mix them by applying a very strong fire, and immediately the white will turn into a red color so transcendent that you yourself will immediately marvel that so much has been granted by God to human hands and ingenuity.

Now, however, you have the four elements united together; you have the Fire of the human race <note>original: "$\triangle$ humani gris," likely an abbreviation for "humani generis"</note>. If you wish to open this through putrefaction <term>putrefaction|the process of breaking down a substance through gentle heat to release its inner virtues</term>, you will advance it to such perfection that it can cure all ailments that befall the human body, and preserve it from death as long as it possesses even a little radical moisture <term>radical moisture|the essential, life-sustaining fluid thought to be innate to all living things</term>. It will perform other wonders which almost exceed belief, and which are therefore left to be discussed by the practitioner.

...I leave these things to be examined. All these operations must be carried out before fermentation, for that matter is given its specific character by the substance with which it is fermented.

To ferment your elixir, to the end that it may be multiplied, proceed in this way:
**Take** <note>original: R.</note> equal parts <note>original symbol: oo</note> of common **Mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note> and **mix** <note>original: mf</note> them with **Tin** <note>original symbol: ♃</note>, the purest **Gold** <note>original symbol: ☉</note>, and a portion of the **medicine** <note>original symbol: ♃</note>; it is thus turned into perfect Mercury, which you will use for **projection** <note>the act of casting the elixir upon a base metal to transform it</note> upon metal, and it shall become **Gold** <note>original symbol: ☉</note>.
For the white work: **Take** equal parts of common **Mercury** and **mix** with **Tin**, the purest **Silver** <note>original symbol: ☽</note>, and a portion of the **medicine**; and thus you will have both elixirs perfectly fermented.

When, however, you have reached the desired end of this mastery, and you have seen all the miracles which will be revealed to you in its progress and happy completion, consider with what thanksgivings you should follow that supreme Divinity. It is by His guidance and light that your intellect has attained such sublime and Divine mysteries. Even if you were continually humbled to the dust and ashes, and reduced yourself <insert>to</insert> poverty (which is impossible) through duties of charity, not even yet a hundredth,

---

not even, I say, a thousandth part would you repay of that supreme goodness, to which you are so greatly bound. Your duties, whatever they might finally be, can no more be measured than a drop of **water** <note>original: H2O/$\nabla$</note> compared to the Ocean.

### Brief Preface of George Ripley to the Pupil of Alchemy.

<margin>
**Gold** <note>original symbol: ☉</note> is gold both externally and internally. **Silver** <note>original symbol: ☽</note>, however, is **Lead** <note>original symbol: ♄</note> externally and **Gold** internally.
</margin>
First, understand what the notable Bernard <note>likely Bernard Trevisan, a 15th-century alchemist</note> says: there is in **Lead** <note>original symbol: ♄</note> both gold and silver potentially, though not to the sight. These two are left by nature only half-cooked <term>concocted: a term for the natural "ripening" of metals in the earth</term>; therefore, our craft must accomplish and perfectly fulfill the rest of their cooking, by the help of a ferment taken from perfect **Gold** <note>original symbol: ☉</note> and **Silver** <note>original symbol: ☽</note>. For a little bit of the stable, "fixed" substance will draw much of the other "unfixed" substance into the perfection of Gold and Silver. Just as roasted salt helps water to do in a short time above the earth what nature cannot do within the earth in many years, so shall you conceive that Saturn <note>Lead</note> contains great secrets within him. For in him is "Quicksilver"—clean, pure, and fragrant—which was not brought to completion by Nature. That quicksilver is the base of the precious medicine for both metals and for the human body, called the **Elixir of Life**. Of this **Mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note> the Philosopher <note>original: Pter/Pher</note> means when he says: "In Mercury is whatever the Wise seek."

<margin>
$\text{*}$ In other manuscripts: lead.
**Lead** <note>original symbol: ♄</note>
</margin>

# Fol. 58.

The Wise; body from this, soul, and spirit, are drawn without care; and with this Mercury <note>original: ☿</note> in which is the Philosopher’s <note>original: <term>Phess</term></note> fire <note>original: △</note> ever burning at one heat within the glass and not without the glass, having (by its great virtuous attractive power to dissolve perfect Gold <note>original: ☉</note> and Silver <note>original: ☽</note> and to reduce them into their original or first matter) you must dissolve your metallic powders <note>original: calces</note> of perfect bodies with that which is the solidification <note>original: congelation</note> of the Spirit. For if you dissolve Philosophically, when the body is dissolved then is the spirit solidified. And to this agrees <term>Raymund</term> <note>Raymund Lull (c. 1232–1315), a major figure in medieval alchemy and logic.</note> saying: "Thus we dissolve gold and silver <margin>* Other manuscripts: by prying nature.</margin> with radical things of their own kind, not yet perfectly finished by nature." <margin>* Otherwise Mercury: we dissolve Gold and Silver with radical things of their own kind not yet perfected by nature but moderately finished.</margin> But in other places he says "radical things" plurally, and not "radical thing" singularly; it is to be understood that there are more than one of these dissolving mercuries. And truly so they are of diverse conditions, though they are both of one kind. For the first Mercury <note>original: ☿</note> is a purging medicine for all uncleanness in a man's body and for metals also; and the other Mercury <note>original: ☿</note> is death to man and health to metals that are unclean, for it alters them into Gold. And this Mercury <note>original: ☿</note>, as Raymund says, is a thing hidden within the body of the <term>green lion</term> <note>A common alchemical symbol for a potent solvent or the mineral antimony.</note>, whose spirit alone multiplies and amplifies the tincture of the ferment in a wondrous way <note>original: cuius solus spus tincturam fermenti multiplicat & ampliat miromodo</note>. Of this he means when he said: "all alchemical gold is made from corrosives and therefore it destroys nature" <note>original: omne aurum alchymicum fit ex corrosiuis & ideo destruit naturam</note>. Wherefore this Mercury <note>original: ☿</note> is named by Raymund: Our Fire <margin>* The Book of Abridgments (Liber Accurtationum).</margin>

<column-break/>

Our Fire <note>The catchword "nr" is an abbreviation for "noster," meaning "our."</note> against Nature. But it happens with this Mercury <note>original: ☿</note> somewhat as it does with other Mercury, which is natural Gold <note>original: ☉</note> and Silver <note>original: ☽</note>, for both are hidden in the middle of her body <margin>* Alternatively: their bodies.</margin>, that is to say, between faint water on the one side and gross earth on the other side, and they are very difficult to obtain without the great craft of Philosophy. And no part will profit us but only the middle part. Because <term>Raymund</term> says: "we take neither from the first things because they are too simple, nor from the last because they are gross and drossy, but from the middle things in which the tincture is" <note>original: neque accipimus de primis quia sunt multum simplicia, neqz de ultimis quia sunt grossa & faculenta, sed de medijs in quibus est tinctura</note>. And this Tincture is a very oil separated from foul Earth and faint water. And therefore Raymund says: "an oily humidity <margin>* An oily humidity is the matter most near to the matter of philosophical quicksilver.</margin> is the matter most near to the matter of our philosophical quicksilver." And though it is so that the bodies in which these (two) Mercuries <note>original: ☿</note> are found are of high price, and are bought at the apothecary's, according to the Philosopher's <note>original: <term>Phess</term></note> saying: "Our Sulfurs are prepared for us by the Apothecaries at a similar price" <note>original: Sulfura nostra sunt nobis ab Apothecarijs parata infimili precio</note>; yet unless you have the craft of separating the Elements, according to the saying of <term>Aristotle</term> in his epistle to <term>Alexander</term> in the book *The Secret of Secrets* <note>original: lib. de secretis secretorum</note>: separate the subtle from the gross, and the thin from the thick, and "when you shall have water from fire <note>original: △</note> and air from fire, fire from earth, then you shall clearly have the art" <note>original: cum habueris aquā ex △ & aerem ex igne, ignem ex terra, tunc habebis plane artem</note>. Otherwise you shall have little profit in your working. Therefore now take heed, for I will plainly unfold to you all the mystery of this science and how you shall first practice with the body to have the <term>Alchemical Elixir</term> <note>original: Elixir alchymicum</note>.

### THE PUPIL OF ALCHEMY. G. RIPLEY.
### Practical Part.

I said before: Our sulfurs are prepared by the Apothecaries <note>original: "Sulphura nra [nostra] sunt ab Apothecarijs parata"</note>. That is to say, those out of which we draw artificially oils and waters more necessary unto us. However, there are more mercuries than two, such as vegetable and animal mercuries, which may be drawn out of certain liquids such as out of blood and egg; both these vegetable and animal mercuries are sovereign for the health of man’s body. But we leave these two at this time until afterwards, and let us show the cause why we rather take these two Sulfurs ordained to us by the apothecaries rather than the others; That is to say, the **red Lion**, and the **green Lion** <note>In alchemy, the "Red Lion" often refers to cinnabar or red lead, while the "Green Lion" usually refers to vitriol or raw mineral ore</note>, which we will not hide, being red lead <note>minium</note>, prepared mineral Antimony, and Roman Vitriol <note>blue or green copper/iron sulfates</note>. Both are of low price. To answer unto this state: the more subtle that the body is, the sooner it will be dissolved; and for that, dissolution must be made by the help of the vegetable mercury, or by the help of one of those that are vegetable. And this vegetable mercury

<column-break/>

may not enter into the body to work it toward dissolution unless the body be made spongy. And no body is so spongy, neither so subtle, as is red Lead and prepared antimony, which is very spongy <margin>* alternate reading: into <br/> * alternate reading: no lead is so spongy nor so subtle as is red lead. <br/> + it is right necessary that we will speed to take red lead or d. prepared which is very spongy. — and + alternate reading: the water</margin> rather than any other lead, which is not so spongy nor subtle. For into that [red lead] it will immediately enter and perform its dissolution as is necessary. As for the second body, which is Roman Vitriol, understand you that it is easier to make separation of elements in that thing which was never fused <note>original: "conflate"</note> into a hard and concocted substance by the fire, than in that which is fused and stripped of its growth-power <note>original: "Vegetability"</note> by melting into metal which is hard, and in respect of the other, completely incapable of increase. For Gold is but the "dropping of the Sun," as Bartholomew <note>likely referring to the medieval encyclopedist Bartholomeus Anglicus</note> says when it is engendered in the mine. And although the Earth has a marvelous tincture of redness, yet the tincture is infected with earthiness called its "original filth," which hinders it from being Gold. Therefore does Raymond <note>Referring to Raymond Lull, a famous though often pseudonymous alchemical authority</note> say of it: "Act so that the earthly virtues do not take the place of the heavenly virtues, and you will have a good thing in the Oil, for indeed you choose two things that are less viscous, etc." <note>original: "fac quod virtutes terrestres non obtineant locum sup [super] virtutes celestes & habes rem bonam in Oleo quia nam elegis duo quæ sunt minus viscosa &c"</note> This, that is to say <margin>* alternate reading: namely Blue vitriol and azure.</margin> * Green vitriol, etc. <margin>* alternate reading: the thing complexioned, <br/> * alternate reading: of that fully intracted. <br/> * alternate reading: foul.</margin>

Let us then begin to practice upon these two aforesaid, which of all others pertaining to this Science are most profitable, * more special in this way, since there are no two bodies known of such Tincture and price. <margin>* alternate reading: in this way</margin>

-> For the first <-

<summary>George Ripley transitions from alchemical theory to practice, identifying "red lead" (antimony) and "Roman vitriol" as the primary "Sulfurs" for the work. He explains that these materials are preferred because their "spongy" and "unfused" nature allows the vegetable mercury to dissolve them more effectively than hard, melted metals.</summary>
<keywords>George Ripley, Pupil of Alchemy, Practical Part, red lead, antimony, Roman vitriol, green lion, red lion, vegetable mercury, dissolution, alchemical tincture</keywords>

--- Page 68 ---
<meta>This page continues the alchemical instructions from the previous manuscript fragment, focusing on the preparation of mineral spirits and the "Green Lion" solvent.</meta>

### For the first step: Take as much fine red lead <note>original: "j. antimony preparate," referring to one part prepared antimony</note> as you wish, and as many gallons of strong distilled vinegar <note>noted as seven parts aqua vitae</note> as there are pounds—one gallon to every pound of distilled vinegar. Let it be put upon the red lead in any earthen vessel that is well-leaded or glazed. Every day for the space of three weeks, stir it strongly with a great staff six or seven times, and keep it covered from dirt. Let it come near no fire for that entire time. When this time has passed, filter away all the clearest liquid, which should be as clear as crystal, into another clean vessel. Put it into a clean pan of Boulogne brass <note>a specific high-quality alloy</note> and set it upon a fire <note>original: $\Delta$</note>. <margin>* alternative: evaporate away all the liquid <note>original: $\nabla$</note> by easy fire.</margin> Evaporate it away with a gentle fire until it becomes very thick at the bottom. Then let it cool, and when it is completely cold (it will be solid), so that you may cut it into chunks with a knife, put eight layers of that mass by weight into a glass vessel. Attach a "head" <note>an alembic lid</note> and seal it as securely as possible with "lute" <note>a sealing paste</note> made of iron scales, flour, and egg whites ground together and applied to linen cloths. Set the glass in <margin>* alternative: closed to the middle about with sand.</margin> sand (and not in ashes) in a furnace up to the middle, and let the sand be two fingers thick under the bottom of the glass. Attach a receiving vessel, but do not seal it until the time that all the weak water has distilled off with a gentle fire. <margin>* alternative: make as many [distillations] as you can, so that it can distill; and when you see the white smoke appear that will make the glass within as white as milk, quickly put...</margin> Proceed as steadily as you can so that it can distill, and when you see the white smoke <note>white fume</note> appear that will make the glass within as white as milk, quickly put... <margin>...forth, then cast away all the weak water.</margin>

<column-break/>

# Process of the Calcination of Gold <note>original: $\odot$</note> separately, likewise.

-> As it is delivered in another manuscript. <-

Take a great quantity of vinegar <note>original: "eysell"</note> distilled through an alembic, into which you shall put a great quantity of green Roman Vitriol <note>iron sulfate</note> to dissolve. When it is dissolved, filter that water and keep it in a glass vessel well stopped. If any part of the vitriol remains <unclear>undissolved</unclear>, dissolve it again in more of the said vinegar. Combine those distilled liquids and distill them through an alembic into a receiver that is well closed so that no air may escape. Use only a gentle fire <note>original: "lent $\Delta$"</note> and distill all the liquid that will come from it, but beware that the dissolved vitriol does not congeal into a dry substance; ensure it remains liquid and soft. Then put the liquid into an alembic with its receiver well-closed together and set it in a furnace of calcination under a temperate fire. Do this for fear of breaking the glass and to draw off the strong humidity of the vitriol. When the white liquid is distilled and the alembic begins to turn red, change the receiver. Close the second receiver tightly to the pipe of the alembic so that no heat escapes, then increase the fire until the redness of the alembic subsides. Then you have in the receiver the **red blood of the Green Lion** <note>a potent acidic spirit used to dissolve gold</note>. This contains within itself the four elements of good flavor and good smell. Keep this "blood" well in a glass vessel tightly stopped so that no heat <note>original: $\Delta$</note> enters it, and put it into hot horse dung <note>used as a natural, steady heat source for slow digestion</note> to purify, changing the dung every five days until fifteen or eighteen days have passed. Then it is ready to be...

<summary>This page provides detailed instructions for two alchemical preparations: the creation of a crystalline substance from red lead or antimony, and the distillation of "the blood of the Green Lion" from Roman vitriol. These processes were essential steps in preparing solvents capable of "opening" or calcining gold for further operations.</summary>
<keywords>alchemy, red lead, antimony, distillation, Boulogne brass, lute, calcination, gold, Green Lion, Roman vitriol, horse dung digestion</keywords>

<margin>The best cement <note>original: Lutum optimum</note>.</margin>

The vessel is closed tightly with a paste or cement made of
Honey, Armenian bole <note>A reddish-brown clay from Armenia used for its sealing properties and as a base in alchemy.</note>, and the powder of the scales of
**Gold** <note>original: ☉</note>, boiled and tempered all together, until all becomes
very thick and black. This cement, if it be well

somewhat black, and will drink up no more liquid.

10 ounces <note>original: ℥ x.</note> of crude **mercury** <note>original: ☿</note> well cleansed, and set it

may increase it infinitely. 1 dram <note>original: ʒ j.</note> of that will

until all the red mercury is turned into the nature of a powder,
red and dry and very "fixed" <note>stable under heat</note>. Then circulate it with
as much more mercury, and do so until it remains 
very thick like oil, somewhat black, and will dry
no more. Then take one dram <note>original: ʒ j.</note> of this oily Elixir and cast
it upon ten ounces <note>original: ℥ x.</note> of raw mercury, well cleansed, and set it on
hot embers until it begins to smoke and is thoroughly
hot, and soon it shall turn into a red powder and dry.

Then put it into a circulatory vessel as you did before with the **sulfur** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note> in making the thick oil, while it sits as before in the portion of the **mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note> reserved for that purpose; then your Elixir is increased by that much. # Now concerning the white work: 
<note>The 'white work' or *albedo* is the stage in alchemy aimed at producing the Silver Elixir.</note>
You must distill your red mercury three times with a gentle **fire** <note>original symbol: 🜂</note>, always discarding the dregs, and then your mercury will be as white as any milk. This is the **Virgin’s Milk** <note>original: *Lac Virginis*</note>, the **whitened solvent** <note>original: *menstruum dealbatum*</note>, and the **quicksilver** <note>original: *argentum vivum*</note> whose **spirit shall be tempered** <note>original: *Spūm temperabitur*</note>. In this substance, through the process of circulation, create an oil from the powder <note>original: "Calx"</note> of **silver** <note>original symbol: ☽</note>. Proceed in every way as you did with your red work upon the powder of gold, and you shall have the **white Elixir**, which shall turn all kinds of metals into fine silver at all tests.

And this **Golden Oil**, thoroughly combined by circulation with artificial balm until everything is a clear golden color, shall be **potable gold** <note>original: *aurum potabile*; a legendary medicinal liquid gold.</note> and a white Elixir more precious for human bodies than all the medicines in the world. Here we end the first mercury, which is the **blood of the red Lion** <note>A symbolic name for the refined acidic solvent or the prepared mineral mercury.</note>, for both the white and red works; and here follows the working of the second mercury, which is the **blood of the green Lion** <note>Commonly refers to raw vitriol or unrefined mineral ores.</note>, for both white and red.

## Concerning the second work: 
Take the mercury sublimated from mercury and common **salt** <note>original symbol: ⊖</note>, which you may also buy ready-made from the Apothecaries better and cheaper than you can make it yourself. Take it fresh, of which you will need 20 or 40 pounds in weight if you

# * An addition which I have found in another manuscript.
Now, regarding the second way, **Raymond** <note>Likely referring to Raymond Lull, a 13th-century philosopher to whom many alchemical works were later attributed.</note> taught King Richard a shortcut <note>original: *accurtation*</note> in this manner:

Dissolve the powder of gold and mercury mentioned before in a strong corrosive **water** <note>original symbol: 🜄</note> made of oil and salt according to common practice. When it is dissolved, put it into a circulatory vessel and set it in a **bath** <note>original: B., for *balneum*; a heat-regulating water or sand bath.</note>, sealed until half the water has evaporated and the material at the bottom begins to grow thick. Then seal the circulatory vessel as securely as you can and, with a gentle fire in a hot furnace, fix the powder. By dissolving, evaporating, and fixing it ten times, the water will then become oily, so that it will absorb no more of the corrosive water nor return to powder, but remain as a very thick oil. This oil will fix mercury and turn other metals into perfect gold—except for use in human bodies, which requires more work. I know of no works in this science as true as these two. Therefore, let this suffice, with honor, praise, and thanks given unto God.

## THE BOSOM BOOK, of George Ripley. continued

### The whole work of the composition of the Stone and call of the great Elixir, and of the first solution of the gross body.

First take 30 lb weight of <term>Sericon<note>Commonly associated with red lead (minium), used here as the primary raw material for the work.</note></term> which will make 20 lb of gum or near thereabout, if it be well dissolved and the vinegar very good
<margin>
* Alternatively: in a gallon of twice distilled vinegar.
</margin>
and distilled pound thereof in his gallon of cold vinegar first twice distilled; and as it standeth in dissolution in his glass vessels stir it about with a clean stick very often every day, and the oftener the better; and when it is well molten to the bottom, then filter over the said Liquors 3 several times, which keep close covered, and cast away the feces <note>dregs or waste</note>, for that is superfluous filth which must be removed, and enters not into the work but is called <term>condemned earth<note>original: "terra damnata"; the worthless residue left after an extraction.</note></term>.

### The making of our gum.

Then put all these cold liquors thus filtered into his glass vessels, and set it into a <term>water bath<note>original: "B.M.", abbreviating "Balneum Mariae"; a gentle heating method using hot water.</note></term> to evaporate in a temperate heat which, once done, our Sericon will be coagulated into a green gum called the <term>Green Lion<note>A famous alchemical symbol representing a potent solvent or the raw, acidic mineral state of the matter before purification.</note></term>.

<column-break/>

Folio 67.

Green Lion, which gum dry well, yet beware thou burn not his flowers <note>the volatile essences that rise during heating</note> nor destroy his green.

### The extraction of the <term>solvent<note>original: "menstruue"; a liquid substance used to dissolve or extract materials.</note></term> of <term>antimony<note>Symbol: ♁. In this context, it refers to the material of the "Green Lion."</note></term>, or blood of the Green Lion.

Then take out the said gum and put it into a strong retort of glass very well <term>luted<note>Sealed with "lute," a clay or cement used to make vessels airtight.</note></term> and place it in the furnace, and under it at the first make a sober <term>fire<note>Symbol: 🜂.</note></term> and shortly you shall see a faint water issue forth, let it waste away; but when you see a white smoke or fume issue forth, then put to a receiver of glass, which must have a very large belly and the mouth no wider than may well receive into it the neck of the retort, which close well together that no fume issue forth of the receiver, and then increase your <term>fire<note>Symbol: 🜂.</note></term> by little and little, until the fume which issues will be reddish, and then continue the <term>fire<note>Symbol: 🜂.</note></term> until no more fume will issue forth. And when it leaves "bleeding" <note>dripping liquid</note> ease the <term>fire<note>Symbol: 🜂.</note></term> by little and little, and when all things are cold, then take away the receiver and close it fast suddenly so that the spirits vanish not away, for this liquor is called the blessed liquor: which liquor keep close stopped in a glass until hereafter. Then look in the neck of the retort and therein you shall find a white hard rime as it were the congelation of a frosty vapor, or much like <term>sublimate<note>A solid substance produced by heating a material until it vaporizes and then allowing it to settle as a solid again.</note></term>, which gather with diligence and keep that apart, for therein are contained great secrets, which shall be showed hereafter, after the great work is ended.

...ally, the dregs remaining in the retort and which are blackish, like unto soot; these dregs are called our **Dragon** <note>In alchemy, the "Dragon" often represents the crude, unrefined matter or the dark dregs left after distillation.</note>. Calcine one pound or more of these dregs at your pleasure in a fervently hot **fire** <note>original: alchemical symbol for fire (triangle pointing up)</note> in a potter’s or glassmaker's furnace, or in a wind furnace, until it becomes a calx <note>A powder produced by roasting ore or minerals.</note> as white as snow. ### The calcination of the Black dregs, called the Black Dragon.

Then take all the rest of the aforesaid black dregs or dragon, and spread it somewhat thin upon a clean marble, or other fire-stone, and put to the one side thereof a burning coal; the fire will glide through the dregs within half an hour and calcine them into a **citrine** <note>A lemon-yellow or orange-yellow color, marking a specific stage of chemical change.</note> color, very glorious to behold.

### The solution of the said dregs.

<margin>* otherwise: three times as before, and then evaporate it to a gum again.</margin>
Then dissolve the said citrine dregs in such distilled **water** <note>original: alchemical symbol for water (triangle pointing down)</note> as you did before, and then filter it likewise, and afterward make that gum again. Then draw out of it more of the **dragon's blood**, and repeat this work in all points as before, until you have either brought all, or the most part of the dregs, into the natural and blessed **Liquor**. Put all of this Liquor to the first liquor or menstruum <note>A solvent used in alchemy to dissolve solids.</note> called the **Green Lion’s blood**, and set that liquor then all together in a glass vessel for 14 days in **putrefaction** <note>original: putrefactiō; a stage of fermentation or breaking down of the substance.</note>. Afterward proceed to the separation of **Elements**, for now you have all the **water** <note>original: alchemical symbol for water</note> of the stone in this **water** <note>original: alchemical symbol for water</note> blessed liquor, which before lay hidden in the dregs, which the dregs all this time did marvelously hide.

### The separation of the Elements, whereof the first is the air and is also accounted the Ardent water and the water attractive.

Then put all the said putrified menstruum into a still of fine Venice glass fit for the quantity of it; put on the **alembic** <note>An early chemical still consisting of two vessels connected by a tube.</note> and close it to the still with a fine linen cloth dipped in the white of an egg. Then set it in a **Bain-Marie** <note>original: B: M: (Balneum Mariae); a water bath used for gentle, even heating.</note> and attach the receiver, which must be of a great length so that the spirit does not breathe out again. With a very temperate heat, separate the Elements one from another; the Element of **air** will issue forth first, which is an **oil**.

### Our ardent water or water attractive is thus made.

When all the first Element is distilled, then in another still fit for it, **rectify** <note>To purify a liquid by repeated distillation.</note> it; that is to say, distill it over seven separate times, and until it will burn a linen cloth clean up that is dipped in it when it is put to the flame. This is then called our **Ardent water** rectified, and is also called our **water attractive**; keep it very closely stopped, for otherwise its spirit, which is very subtle, will vanish away.

### The flood or water of the stone.

Then draw out the flood or **water** <note>original: alchemical symbol for water</note> of the stone by itself in another receiver; the liquor will be somewhat white. Draw it with a very **gentle fire** <note>original: "lent" (slow) fire, accompanied by the symbol for fire</note> in a **Bain-Marie** until there remains in the bottom of the still a thick oily substance like unto liquid pitch. Keep this water by itself in a suitable glass, very closely stopped.

<margin>* otherwise: oil of substance like unto other.</margin>

### Our man's blood is thus rectified and made.

Then put our **ardent water** upon that black and liquid matter; stir them well together and let it stand well-covered for 3 hours. Then distill it again with a moist **gentle fire** <note>original: alchemical symbol for fire</note> of **Baths** <note>Likely referring to the Bain-Marie or water bath.</note> and boiled three times, and then it is called **man's blood rectified**, which

<margin>* Air * oil.</margin>
...workers in the secrets of Nature do so seek, and so you have the Elements exalted in the virtue of their Quintessence <note>The 'fifth essence' or purest state of a substance</note>, namely the flood that is water and <margin>* Air * oil.</margin> Fire. For this blood, be it kept for a season.

# The oil of Fire <note>Symbol: 🜂</note>.
Then put the flood or Water <note>Symbol: 🜄</note> upon the soft and black matter and let them be well mingled together, and then distill it: whole until there remain in the bottom an earth more dry and black which is the earth of the stone. Save the oil which is Jupiter <note>Symbol: ♃; likely referring to tin or a specific stage of the metal's transformation</note> for a season, closely stopped in a vial.

# The fiery Jupiter <note>Symbol: ♃</note>.
Then beat this black earth into powder and mingle it with man's blood <note>In alchemy, 'man's blood' often refers to a red mineral or a specific chemical state rather than biological blood</note> and so let it stand 3 hours. After that, distill it on ashes with a good Fire <note>Symbol: 🜂</note> and repeat this work 3 times. Then it shall be called water of the rectified fire, and so you have 3 of the Elements exalted into the virtue of Quintessence, namely Water, Air, and Fire.

# The Earth:
Then calcine the black and dry earth in a furnace of reverberation <note>A furnace that reflects heat onto the material being heated</note> until it becomes a very fine white Calx <note>A powdery residue left after heating a metal or mineral</note>.

# The water of life which is our Moon <note>Symbol: ☽</note> and Mercury <note>Symbol: ☿</note> the Lunary.
Then mingle with this white calx the fiery water and distill it with a strong Fire <note>Symbol: 🜂</note> as before, and calcine the earth again (the remainder in the bottom of the still), and then distill it again with a strong Fire as before and again calcine it; and this distill and calcine 7 times, and until all the substance of the calx be lifted up by the Limbeck <note>Alembic; a distillation apparatus</note>. Then you have the water of life rectified and made indeed spiritual, and so you have the 4 Elements exalted in the virtue of their Quintessence. This water will dissolve all bodies, and putrefy them and purge them, and this is our Moon <note>Symbol: ☽</note> and our Mercury <note>Symbol: ☿</note> Lunary. Whosoever thinks that there is any other water than this is ignorant and a fool and shall never be able to come to the effect.

# The oil which is our red Mercury <note>Symbol: ☿</note> and the Element of Fire <note>Symbol: 🜂</note>.
The flood with the oil previously reserved shall be distilled in a most slow Fire <note>original: "lent 🜂"</note> of the Maria's Bath <note>B. M. or Balneum Mariae; a water bath used for gentle heating</note>. The red oil which remains in the bottom shall be diligently kept by itself, for it is the Element of Fire <note>Symbol: 🜂</note> and the Sun <note>Gold</note> of the Moon <note>Silver</note>. It shall be rectified again, and the same work repeated until no more of the said Lunary <note>A silver-like substance</note> will remain in it.

# The work of Putrefaction.
When all the elements be thus separated, then take the white calcined feces <note>Residue or dregs</note> first of all reserved, called the Sun <note>Symbol: ☉</note> and put so much thereof into a chemical vessel <note>original: "Kemya"</note> as will scarcely fill half the glass, and thereupon pour so much ardent water <note>Strong distilled alcohol or acid</note> rectified as may just well cover the Calx. Which being done, immediately stop close the glass with a blind head <note>A cap for a vessel without an outlet</note>, and set it in a cold place until the calx has drunk up all the liquor, which it will do in 7 days or 8 days, until the same calx will drink no more, but stands liquid still. Then seal up the glass with Hermes' seal <note>An airtight seal made by melting the glass neck of the vessel</note> and set it in the Maria's Bath in temperate heat for putrefaction <note>The breaking down of the substance through warmth and moisture</note>.

# The digestion of the white stone.
<margin>another word: guard.</margin>
Then in that temperate bath let your glass stand kept unremoved for the space of fully 150 days, and until the stone within the glass becomes first russet, afterwards green, and after that very white like unto the eyes of fishes, which is the Sun's Sulphur of Nature <note>The active, masculine principle in alchemy</note> flowing and not evaporating in Fire <note>Symbol: 🜂</note>. And this white stone is ready to be fermented.

Know the true weight of the said half, and let one half be reserved for the white work <note>The "White Work" or *Albedo* is the second major stage of alchemy, aimed at creating silver</note>; the other half should be put into a glass and sealed up again with a Hermetic seal <note>An airtight seal, traditionally made by melting the glass neck of a flask</note>. Then, move
<margin>* Otherwise: incineration.</margin>
the glass to the Sinevicon <note>Likely a specialized furnace or a specific level of sustained heat</note>, which is somewhat hotter than the fire <note>Symbol: Δ</note>. Let it stand there, undisturbed in that digestion, until it becomes red and of a purple color. Thus, you have the red stone also ready to be fermented.

## The Preparation of the ferment for the White.

Then take silver <note>Symbol: ☽</note>, well purified from all other metals and any filth that may be joined with it. Dissolve it in as much of our Lunary—which is our mercury <note>Symbol: ☿</note>—as the quantity of the silver is, and in no greater quantity, as near as you can manage. Set it upon warm ashes, closely covered, and when it is thoroughly dissolved, the entire liquid will be green. Then rectify <note>Purify a liquid by repeated distillation</note> it, cleaning it from that state again twice or thrice, so that no drop of the mercury is left with it. Then seal up the "oil of silver" in a chemical flask and set it in a water bath <note>Original: "B." or *Balneo Mariae*</note> to putrefy <note>A process of slow digestion or decomposition using gentle heat</note> until it shows all colors, and finally becomes crystalline white. This is then the white "ferment of ferments."

## The fermentation of the white stone.

Then put that half of the white stone previously reserved for the white work into a suitable glass. Know its weight, and put as much of the said silver ferment into the glass with the stone as would equal a fourth part of the said stone. Once the glass is well sealed with lute <note>A clay or cement used to seal alchemical vessels</note>, fix them together in a fixing vessel under the fire <note>Symbol: Δ</note>, which will
<meta>catchword: be well</meta>

<column-break/>

be well finished in two or three days.

## The Inceration of the white stone.

When they are thus fixed together and have become one very fine powder, "incerate" it—that is to say, soak it with the white oil of our stone, which is our Lunary. Do this by pouring it on drop by drop until the stone is oiled, and then congeal it. Soak it again and, in this manner, repeat this work until the stone will flow in the fire <note>Symbol: Δ</note> like wax when it is placed upon a plate of "Fiery Gold" <note>Symbol: ☉; likely a heated gold or copper plate used for testing</note>. It must not evaporate. Congeal it until it is hard, white, and as transparently clear as crystal. Then it is the "Medicine of the Third Degree" and the perfect white stone, transmuting all metallic bodies—and especially copper <note>Symbol: ♀</note> and iron <note>Symbol: ♂</note>—into pure and perfect silver <note>Symbol: ☽</note>.

## The Preparation of the red ferment.

In the same way, take gold <note>Symbol: ☉</note>, first very purely purged of all other metals that may be joined with it, with ten parts of mercury <note>Symbol: ☿</note>. Then dissolve it into our mercury or "dissolving liquor" as you did previously with the silver. When it is perfectly dissolved, the liquid will be lemon-yellow <note>Original: "citrine"</note>. Then, in the same manner, rectify it again (the gold and the mercury or dissolving liquid) and seal up the oil of gold ferment in a chemical flask
<margin>* it will likewise</margin>
in the furnace <note>Original: "in foris," likely referring to the heat of a furnace or external heat source</note> and set it in a water bath to putrefy. It must become black and must stand undisturbed in digestion until it becomes white. Then, move it to a stronger fire <note>Symbol: Δ</note> without opening the glass, and keep it until it changes colors and becomes lemon-yellow. This is then also the "ferment of ferments" for the red work <note>The "Red Work" or *Rubedo* is the final stage of the Great Work, creating the Philosopher's Stone capable of making gold</note>.

### The Fermentation of the red stone.
Then take the other half of the stone—which was previously turned red, matured through heat, and set aside for the "red work"—and add enough gold ferment so that it makes up a quarter of the total weight of the stone. Then stabilize them using <term>fire</term> <note>original: $\triangle$</note> in a sealing vessel, just as you did for the white stone. This process will be successfully completed in two or three days.

### The work of inceration.
When the materials are stabilized together and have become a very fine powder, perform "inceration"—that is, saturate it with the red oil of our stone. Then let it thicken and solidify again. Repeat this process of saturating and solidifying until the substance flows and melts like wax, but does not evaporate when placed upon a hot plate of <term>Antimony</term> <note>original: $\unicode{x2641}$, the globus cruciger symbol often used for antimony or the element Earth</note>. At that point, let it thicken until it becomes clear, transparent, and red in color, like a ruby or hyacinth gemstone. This is then a "Medicine of the Third Degree" and the perfect Red Stone, capable of transforming all metals—and especially <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: $\unicode{x263F}$</note>, <term>Antimony</term> <note>original: $\unicode{x2641}$</note>, and <term>Silver</term> <note>original: $\unicode{x263D}$</note>—into gold as pure as any from a natural mine.

### Thus you have the method for making the <term>Philosophic</term> <note>original: $\unicode{x2609}$, the symbol for the Sun or Gold</note> stone, both white and red, which is the great secret of Philosophy. These stones must be kept separately in individual glass containers or clean boxes in a warm place—or at least a dry one—just as you would store sugar. Because they are of such a delicate and oily substance, they are prone to dissolving in any moist environment. Therefore, preserve them as shown here.

->The end of the great work.<-

### SHORTCUT of the great work, which saves half the work and labor in the process. Revealed by Mr. GEORGE RIPLEY.

The white, frosty frost or powder I told you about at the beginning—which is found in the neck of the retort after drawing off the solvent—is like a sublimate. It is the "salt <term>fire</term> <note>original: $\triangle$</note> of Nature," and therefore it requires neither purification nor heat-maturation to become "white." Of this <term>fire</term> <note>original: $\triangle$</note>, take either half or the whole amount, as you wish. Also take as much <term>Silver</term> <note>original: $\unicode{x263D}$</note> ferment (when it is the "ferment of ferments") as may equal a quarter part of the said <term>fire</term> <note>original: $\triangle$</note>. Seal them both together in a <unclear>chemical vessel</unclear> and stabilize them together under <term>fire</term> <note>original: $\triangle$</note> in a sealing vessel, which will be well finished in three days.

When they have become a single, very white, fine powder, then "incerate" it—that is to say, saturate it with the white oil of our stone which has been solidified once. Saturate it and repeat this work, following the same steps as you did for the "white work" in the Great Elixir previously. This <term>fire</term> <note>original: $\triangle$</note> is of the same nature, and in this way, you shall obtain the white work freshly made, saving half the time and labor through this salt; it is not only a precious jewel but also a great secret.

## The multiplication or increase of the
## power and goodness of the aforesaid
## white and red stone.

If you wish to elevate your medicine or stone in quality or goodness, then put your aforementioned Stone <note>original: L., likely for *Lapidem*</note>—the white and red stone—or parts of each into separate glass vials fit for the quantity. Seal the vials well, then hang your glass or glasses in the steam of a <term>Water Bath</term> <note>original: *B. M.* or *Balneum Mariae*</note> so that they do not touch the water. In this warm steam or breath, the stone which was previously solidified in the glass will now dissolve. Then solidify it again upon warm ashes, and dissolve and solidify it once more in this way...

<column-break/>

...and so repeat this work of dissolution and solidification until at last the stone dissolved within the glass will solidify as soon as it comes out of the pot or the Bath and feels the cold air, without any other method of solidification being needed. Note that every time you dissolve and solidify your medicine or stone in this work, its power increases tenfold in its "projection" <note>the final stage where the stone transforms base metals</note>. So, if at the first stage 1 ounce <note>original: ℥ 1</note> will convert 100 ounces, after the second dissolution the same ounce shall convert 1,000; after the third, 10,000; after the fourth, 100,000; and after the fifth, 1,000,000 parts of any imperfect metal into pure and true gold and silver, passing all tests just like that from a natural mine.

---

## The way of Projection.

Because it is very cumbersome to melt a million parts of any metal when you wish to perform "projection," do as follows: Take 100 pounds <note>original: ℔ 100</note> of <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: ☿</note>, first washed with salt and vinegar, and put it into a <term>crucible</term> <note>original: ♁</note> and set it over the <term>Fire</term> <note>original: $\Delta$</note>. When it begins to grow hot, add 1 ounce <note>original: ℥ 1</note> of the elixir or medicine prepared as described above to those 100 parts of cleansed metal, and all of that <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: ☿</note> shall become medicine itself. Then cast 10 pounds <note>original: ℔ 10</note> of that medicine upon another 100 pounds of other washed <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: ☿</note>, and it shall also be converted into medicine. Then cast 1 pound <note>original: ℔ 1</note> of this last solidified <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: ☿</note> upon 100 pounds of other washed <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: ☿</note>, and all that shall be converted...

# Provisions for the Way. G[eorge] R[ipley].

Take <term>calcined Tin (♃)</term> <note>original: "♃. calcinatum"; Tin was associated with the planet Jupiter in alchemical shorthand</note> and place it in a glass vessel; 
pour over it a suitable quantity of <term>vegetable solvent</term> <note>original: "menstrui degetabilis"; likely a typo for "vegetabilis"</note> 
when it has been brought to its full strength, 
distill the vapors from the <term>calx</term> <note>The powdery residue left after heating a metal</note> and replace them again; 
and strain it so many times until the calx is converted into an oil 
or becomes so subtle that it flows like wax 
upon a heated plate and does not evaporate. 
This medicine converts <term>quicksilver (☿)</term> into the most perfect <term>Gold (☉)</term>.

### Another work.
Calcine <term>Tin (♃)</term> as above by <term>Fire (△)</term> alone 
into a white and very subtle calx; once this is done, 
dissolve it, as you know how, in its own corrosive [solvent] and make from it 
a pure <term>Oil</term>. Extract this from the corrosive with <term>burning Water (▽)</term> <note>likely alcohol or a strong acid</note>; 
and then let it be mixed with the Oil made from 
Tin (♃) at the same time, and separate them. 
Then let it be fixed into a Stone, where the <term>marcasite</term> <note>A term often used for iron pyrites or other metallic minerals</note> 
is so brittle, for many days in a slow <term>Fire (△)</term>. 
This Tin (♃) for certain converts ten parts of <term>Quicksilver (☿)</term> into <term>Gold (☉)</term> for 
every work. 
It performs two things: First, that Tin (♃) is that body in 
which there is clean and pure quicksilver 
not yet brought to completion by nature. 
Second, because through the oil of Jupiter, which is called "More-than-Tin" <note>original: "Plus ♃"</note>, it is most easily 
hardened; and the Oil of <term>Gold (☉)</term> fixes all spirits. 
Therefore, you fix that infused spirit which is in 
Jupiter, and it removes its softness and impurity. 
Work with the Spirit of Tin until you are rich, 
for in it is a work of easy handling and 
low expense.

->Elixir of Tin from the Bosom Book.<- <note>original: "Elixir de ♃. B̄orum"; 'B̄orum' is an abbreviation for 'Bosom Book,' a title Ripley gave to his most essential recipes.</note>

<header>ANALYSIS OF THE WORLD.</header>
<page-num>Fol. 74.</page-num>

## Extracts from a certain Manuscript 
## of a Rosicrucian Philosopher.

A thing consists of those substances into which it can be resolved. 
This resolution is illustrated by ice turning into water. 
God Himself first instilled this axiom into Adam: 
**"Dust thou art, and unto dust shalt thou** 
**return."** If then all things can finally be 
resolved into quicksilver, then all things 
must fundamentally consist of such a substance; 
or, this universe is equivalent: 
if things consist of such a substance primarily, 
then they can finally be resolved into the same. 
The proof of this depends upon this (second) 
theorem: Those things which agree 
in a third thing, agree among themselves. 
If therefore metals, minerals, plants, 
and animals, when converted, can be assimilated into 
flesh in the human body, then 
they are the same in the human body, where the 
disposition of parts is converted into a human harmony, 
so that it necessarily follows that the 
first and immediate basis of all these is 
one and the same in kind. It remains therefore 
to be examined which of these consists of parts 
more similar to one another, and whether the 
nourishment of the rest consists of a 
more immediate grade. 
Animals receive their daily coarse, dry [nourishment] 
from animals and plants, but not from 
minerals or metals; nevertheless, they receive 
medicines from them.

<meta>This page continues the "Analysis Mundi" (Analysis of the World) section. While the previous page focused on the practical alchemical transmutation of Tin, this page shifts to a macrocosmic "chain of being." It describes a hierarchical system of nutrition and healing, where each level of existence—from plants up to the Divine Intellect—derives its "coarse food" (physical substance) from the level below it and its "medicine" (spiritual refinement) from the level above it.</meta>

**Plants** receive their daily coarse food from **minerals**, and their medicine from **animals** and **metals**.
**Minerals** receive their daily coarse food from **metals**, such as Oil from <term>Iron (♂)</term>, <term>Tin (♃)</term>, and <term>Copper (♀)</term>; but they receive their medicine from <term>Quicksilver (☿)</term> and **plants**.
**Metals** receive their daily coarse food from <term>Quicksilver (☿)</term> (just as in the sudden transmutation of <term>Quicksilver (☿)</term> into <term>Tin (♃)</term> and <term>Lead (♄)</term>, only the vapor of those metals appears), but they receive their medicine from **minerals**, such as from <term>Quicksilver (☿)</term> and the <term>Sun (☉)</term> <note>Gold</note>.
**Quicksilver** receives its daily coarse food from **vapor**, but its medicine from the **air**.
**Vapor** receives its daily coarse food from the **air**, and its medicine from the **ether**.

<margin>* The Sporades <note>Isolated stars not belonging to a recognized constellation</note></margin>
The **Air** receives its daily coarse food from the **ether**, and its medicine from the * **Sporades**.
The **Ether** receives its daily coarse food from the * **Sporades**, and its medicine from the **greater fixed stars**.
The * **Sporades** receive their daily coarse food from the **greater fixed stars**, and their medicine from the **twelve signs of the zodiac**, and especially from <term>Aries (♈), Taurus (♉), Gemini (♊), and Cancer (♋)</term>.
The **greater fixed stars** receive their daily coarse food

<column-break/>

daily coarse food from the **twelve signs of the zodiac**, and especially from <term>Aries (♈), Taurus (♉), Gemini (♊), and Cancer (♋)</term>; they receive their medicine, however, from the **seven planets**, the <term>Sun (☉)</term> excepted.
The **twelve signs of the zodiac** receive their daily coarse food from the **seven planets**, and their medicine from the <term>Sun (☉)</term>.
The **six planets** <note>The known planets excluding the Sun: Mercury, Venus, Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, and the Moon</note> receive their daily coarse food from the <term>Sun (☉)</term>, and their medicine from the **universal light**.
The **Sun (☉)** receives its daily coarse food from the **universal light**, and its medicine from the **first waters** or the **first Quicksilver**.
The **Light** receives its daily coarse food from the **first Quicksilver**, and its medicine from the <term>earth that was empty and void</term> <note>original Hebrew: תהו ובהו (Tohu va-Vohu); a reference to Genesis 1:2</note>.

The **earth that was empty and void** receives its daily food from the **Light**, and its medicine from the <term>World Soul</term> <note>Anima Mundi: the spiritual essence thought to animate all nature</note>.

### The Universal Spirit receives its daily food
from the intellect, and its medicine from the 
universal mind.
---
### The Universal Intellect receives its daily 
food from the mind, and its medicine from 
the Trinity.

### The Universal Mind receives its daily 
food from the four lower physical realms <note>original: "4. posteriorib. physijs"; referring to the kingdoms of animals, plants, minerals, and metals described on the previous page</note>, and its medicine from GOD, where all things 
begin. To Whom be praise and glory. Amen.
---
From this analysis it is clear that **Quicksilver** <note>original: "☿ s. arg. viu." (argentum vivum)</note> nourishes common metals; mineral metals nourish minerals; minerals nourish plants; and plants nourish animals. Therefore, from quicksilver, as the nearest universal matter, the four aforesaid terrestrial kingdoms consist, and consequently they can be resolved back into quicksilver. However, one must ascend by the same steps by which one descended, unless the secret natural way—which He has reserved for His adopted sons—is known to you. For then you might seize and shorten the course. But lest you think these are dreams, I will recount certain experiments of this age: I know a man who, in the space of one hour upon the palm of his hand, reduced powdered gold leaf into running 
**quicksilver** <note>original: "☿"</note>. What then will happen to other metals? I know those who from 
**blood**

<column-break/>

**Blood** and vegetables likewise have led them into **quicksilver** <note>original: "argentu̅ vivum"</note>. And those who have extracted it in abundance from the air, yet in greater quantity at one time than at another, etc.

---

### Elixir of Quicksilver <note>original: "☿"</note> Alone. G. R.

Put the body which is heavier into **fire** <note>original alchemical symbol: $\triangle$</note> and draw out the "sweat" <note>vapor or distillate</note> with a small vent. Put that same sweat back upon the undissolved part, and draw it out again, and put it back again. Continue in this way for a long time until all the **quicksilver** <note>original: "☿"</note> is dissolved by a very slow **fire** <note>original: $\triangle$m</note> into **virgin’s milk** <term>Lac virginis: a cloudy white alchemical suspension</term>. Putrefy this for 15 days, etc., until you reach the dregs, which, once dried, you should soak with a little of its own **water** <note>original symbol: $\nabla^\text{a}$</note>. Coagulate and fix them together. Soak and coagulate again, continuing until you have a large quantity of **water** <note>original: $\nabla^\text{a}$</note> contracted with its own **mercury** <note>original: "mer", likely meaning the essential core or mercury of the substance</note>.

Then, with the saved part of the non-fixed water: Reduce the **water** <note>original symbol: $\nabla$</note> into a pure crystalline substance, and coagulate that into the **Elixir**.

You can also soak and wash the dregs of imperfect metallic bodies with clear **Water of Life** <note>original: "aqua Vta" (Aqua Vitae)</note> (which is then called **permanent water** <term>Aqua permanens: a stabilized solvent that does not easily evaporate</term>), whitening them and melting them into true **Silver** <note>original alchemical symbol: ☽</note>.

One part of the coagulated Elixir "runs" upon <note>transmutes</note> 100 parts of **quicksilver** <note>original: "☿"</note>, and one part of that in turn runs upon 100 parts of the substance of an imperfect body to become the purest **Gold** <note>original alchemical symbol: ☉</note>.

---

-> ℞ <note>Take</note> the **Menstruum** <term>Menstruum: a powerful solvent used to extract essences</term> which coagulates **quicksilver** <note>original: "☿"</note> <-
-> into **Silver** <note>original: "☽"</note>. It is the best and the secret of all <-
-> secrets. <-

Circulate the strongest red wine with vegetables.

with our vegetables for 120 days <note>original: "$\odot$"; symbol for the sun, representing a day</note> by continuous rotation in Mary’s Bath <note>original: "MB" or "Mariae Balneum"; a gentle water bath</note>. And afterwards, let only its most pure spirit be passed through a still, to which should be added pure **gold** <note>original: "$\odot \underline{a}$" (Aurum)</note> made without corrosive agents, and let them circulate together for another 100 days. Then it is a **Water** <note>original: "$\nabla$"</note> of the nature of the **Basilisk**. Because just as the Basilisk kills a man in an instant by its gaze alone, so this Water, when applied to **Mercury** <note>original: "☿"</note> without external **heat** <note>original: "$\Delta$" (fire)</note>, freezes it almost suddenly into the best **Silver** <note>original: "☽"</note>. And note that if the "fire" of Celandine <note>original: "$\Delta \underline{a}$ de Lidoniæ"; likely a distilled essence of the herb Chelidonium</note> or the "fire" of the flowers of **Antimony** <note>original: "♁"</note> is added to it after the first circulation—and without the gold and silver being circulated together for 10 days—the mercury will be much better frozen into a stony substance. Once frozen in this way, it is immediately fixed <note>made resistant to heat</note> even without that antimony, as you know.

### Elixir of Life. G[eorge] R[ipley].

Make a **Pelican** <note>a specialized circulatory vessel with tubes that return condensed vapors to the base</note> one cubit in height, and fill a fourth part with well-rectified vegetable menstruum <note>a solvent, usually highly distilled alcohol</note>. Put inside well-crushed spices, and let them be the most precious that can be found. Seal the vessel hermetically and place it in Mary’s Bath at such a degree of heat that only the vegetable spirit can circulate. Continue this circulation at this tempered degree of **heat** <note>original: "$\Delta$"</note> until our wine is rendered as thick as oil, so that its mercury <note>its volatile spirit</note> no longer rises. And so you will have the essence of **Antimony** <note>original: "♁"</note> fixed in its own "heaven," more precious than all gold and silver for the sake of health. This work requires a long 
<meta>catchword: rit (time)</meta>

<column-break/>

Fol. 77

time, for if it is accelerated with too much heat, it will never be led to the proper end.

### Virtues of this Quintessence.

1. Open its vessel, and you will perceive a scent such as no one has ever achieved or smelled in this life.
2. Make a circle from this Quintessence upon a table, and place a venomous animal or a serpent inside the circle; it will die there and will never cross the boundaries of the circle.
3. It heals every infirmity and renews youth.
4. If you fix **Silver** <note>original: "☽"</note> with this Elixir of Quintessence, it will produce that precious **potable gold** <note>aurum potabile; a legendary medicinal liquid gold</note> which heals leprosy and the plague.
5. When **gold** <note>original: "$\odot$"</note> and **Mercury** <note>original: "☿"</note> are fixed by way of precipitation, do so in the Quintessence with this wine.
6. But truly, it will succeed better if, instead of the precipitate, we take **Sericon** <note>a red alchemical lead or earth</note> and fix it with this wine; for then it will be the elixir of metals and of life, for it will freeze imperfect **Mercury** into **gold** <note>original: "$\odot^{\text{m}}$"</note> in the blink of an eye.
7. Furthermore, the Quintessence for metals will be better if it is made from **Silver** <note>original: "☽"</note> and **Antimony** <note>original: "$\delta$"</note> alone instead of this wine.
8. One drop of this Elixir of Life, cast into colored **Water** <note>original: "$\nabla$"</note>, renders it a most excellent potable liquor, which will be most healthful for those who travel in hot regions during the summer.

### $\nabla$ Ripley’s Composite Water.
<note>$\nabla$ is the symbol for Water.</note>

There is also a **Water** made clean and purely finished, 
about which **Maria the Prophetess** <note>Maria Prophetissa, or Mary the Jewess, was a legendary foundational figure in alchemy credited with inventing the water-bath (balneum Mariae).</note> says: make your **Water** 
become a "running water" from two *zacbet* <note>original: "zacbetem." Likely a reference to a type of salt or alum used as a flux.</note> 
cleansed by the vegetable [process] divinely elaborated; this is 
circulated together into one crystalline **Water** which 
has a cohesive weight and the property of glass. 
**Note:** — kill [the substance] upon the fixed body which is from the 
heart of the **Sun** <note>$\odot$ is the symbol for Sol, or Gold.</note>; that is, upon **Gold**, and make from them an **Elixir**. 
**Raymond Lull** <note>Raymond Lull (c. 1232–1315) was a major philosopher to whom many alchemical works were later attributed.</note> says, when you are in the **Moon** <note>$\leftmoon$ is the symbol for Luna, or Silver.</note>, 
slightly heated (whose power thus dissolves), it is necessary 
that the **Water** of the vegetable **Moon** be animated, because otherwise 
it can dissolve nothing; and this is the **Water** and the **Mercury** <note>$\mathcal{M}$ is used here as an abbreviation for Mercury or the "Spirit."</note>: or 
it is the [spirit] of all *metals*, whose virtue you need, 
by which pearls are reformed. 
This vegetable **Water**, composed by reason of the **Moon**, 
immediately dissolves every body, and by the power of its 
vegetability, it revivifies every body— 
or by attraction, it makes an **Oil** <note>$\circ\!\circ$ is the symbol for Oleum (Oil).</note> of every body and **Moon**. 
It draws to itself its like, namely the **Moon** [silver] of the body. 
Regarding this **Water**, **Raymond** says in the compendium called 
*The Art of Transmutation* to **King Robert**: "Know, 
O Most Serene King, that the Stone is not except from those 
things, namely from the vegetable and mineral. And therefore the 
ancient Philosophers said: The Stone is composed of 
only one single thing, i.e., the **Water**. And understand 
that to join two extremes, it is necessary 
that there be a thing sharing the nature of both extremes. 
This **Water** is our pure and natural **Fire** <note>$\triangle$ is the symbol for Fire.</note> 
because the dissolved **Soul** <note>$\Omega$ is the symbol for the Anima or Soul (Sulfur).</note> of the fixed body coagulates 
into a glorious **Water**."

<margin>
**Gold** <note>$\odot$</note>: a body which is heavier than **Silver**. It is the alchemical body. And draw off its sweat, with a small suitable vent so it begins to ascend; and (if it increases) clean the receiver and 15 <note>$\mathcal{X}\mathcal{V}$</note> abstracted from a suitable thick extracted **Oil**, for you will have as much tincture as **Oil**. Then make the **Water** imbibe a large quantity of **Water** by circulating and afterwards by sublimating with a strong **Fire** so that it ascends...
</margin>

<column-break/>

...through the sides of the vessel, and is fixed by itself without dregs and redness with **Fire**. 
First, with the **Oil** of **Gold** or **Silver**, it makes your elixirs; similarly this **Oil** 
fixes the aura of **Mercury** <note>$\mathcal{Q}$ is the symbol for Quicksilver/Mercury.</note> and of the **Soul**. If you wish to work for the 
"white" [the silver stage], dissolve and sublimate the aforesaid and 
fix it in its own **Water**, and congeal it in the same way; 
likewise it fixes **Mercury** and the **Soul** of the **amalgam** <note>aaa is the standard abbreviation for amalgam.</note>. 
If you have in the **Soul** whatever the wise man seeks... 
Truly, the **Oil** of the **Soul** is made thus: Make a corrosive of **Gold** and **Mercury**. 
* Do not put more than one **ounce** <note>$\mathcal{E}$ is the symbol for Uncia (Ounce).</note> in the **Soul** and all the **Mercury** 
together, and [add] with a slow **Fire**. **Water** in which is the **Soul**, and make an **Oil**. 
Combine that **Oil** with the **Oil**. Make the **Tincture** of **Mercury** 
from these gums; there are two gums which are made from the 
vegetable Stone: whence **Maria** [says],

> The gum with the two [others] fixes the fugitive in the depths: 
> the highest elements rectified and not yet combustible, 
> but they either fly away or remain.

The **Soul of Gold** <note>$\odot\Omega$</note> is said to be from the stone, whose shadow is the **Water** 
in which it is cleansed. 
Let the vessel be closed in the philosophical manner <note>original: "more Psico." Likely meaning Hermetically sealed.</note>. At the time of 
rotation, let it be placed in the moist until whiteness, so that what 
is subtle and rare **Mercury** ascends to the top of the vessel, while 
what is thick and coarse remains below. The decoction 
of the Stone lasts through the center for at least fifty days. 
Of the non-fixed part, it is necessary to have a large 
quantity for the soul-joining <note>original: "$\Omega$ionem," a contraction for union or sulfur-interaction.</note> of the Stone under the 
first fixation. The first dregs from which this **Water** is extracted 
must be cast away at the beginning of the work, and our **Water**, 
which being placed upon its **Gold**, begins to bubble up, 
planting and [strengthening] with a more subtle **Water**.

### Secret Citrine Oil of Hermes. <note>$\mathcal{H}$ j.</note>

Grind the **Gum** made from **Sericon** <note>Sericon usually refers to Red Lead or Minium.</note> by means of 
**Hermes** [or the Salt] with as much evaporated **Water**, and first with 
a slow **Fire** evacuate the **Water**, then with a stronger [fire] receive 
the **Oil**, which separate from the **Water** until you have pure **Oil**; 
and thus what is fixed upon the **amalgam** is made: 1 part **Silver**, 
**Mercury**, **Silver**, **Soul**, **Fire** to the weight of all.

<meta>Continuity: This page transitions from the general discussion of "Vegetable Water" to the specific preparation of the "Philosophical Earth," a key stage in the Great Work attributed here to "G.P." (likely George Ripley). It describes the separation of the elements and the "purgatory" or purification of the base matter.</meta>

# Philosophical Earth. G.P.

<margin>$\odot$ <note>$\odot$ is the symbol for the Sun or Gold.</note></margin> Concerning the leaf of **Water** <margin>* leaf of Alchemy</margin> and the brother of **Water**, which is nothing else than **Water**.
**Jupiter** <note>$\text{\jupiter}$ is the symbol for Tin, often representing a specific stage of purification.</note> and **Fire** <note>$\triangle$ is the symbol for Fire.</note> from **Water** are most precious, and you must be prudent in choosing this **Water**. If, therefore, you desire to make the true **Elixir**, see that you extract it from the **Earth** <note>The symbol here is an inverted triangle with a horizontal bar, representing Earth.</note>, from a <gloss>or</gloss> beautiful **Water**, subtle, pure, and good. Imbue this with the **Water** of the grove <note>The "water of the grove" likely refers to a specialized acidic solvent or "mercurial water" used in the process.</note>. For in this **Water**, **Salt** <note>$\oplus$ is the symbol for Salt or Vitriol.</note> is to be dissolved 3 <gloss>parts, that is, without fire.</gloss>
When this is done, separate the subtle from the thick, and evaporate it into a gum in the likeness of pitch. Distill that **Water** twice, which is our **Water of Life** <note><term>Aqua Vitae</term>: Literally "water of life," a highly concentrated or distilled spirit used as a solvent.</note> and our **Menstruum** <note><term>Menstruum</term>: A term for a solvent that can dissolve a solid body.</note>. After its extraction, there comes a **Fire** as red as blood and full of fury. When this too is extracted, there will remain at the bottom a **Earth** as black as a foul thing and as heavy as metal, in which **Earth**, indeed, a great secret is hidden. For it is the mother of all things.
Afterward, it is necessary to pass into **Purgatory** <note>In alchemy, "purgatory" refers to the stage of purification or calcination where impurities are burned away.</note> so that it may endure the punishments suitable to it, until it becomes shining in the **Sun** <margin>$\dagger$</margin>
<margin>X</margin> then it obtains the mastery. This a miraculous thing will accomplish in 3 <gloss>or</gloss> 4 hours. When this is done, you shall give to this **Earth** to <margin>* leaf of Water</margin>
drink our **Water** so that it may become most white. Afterward, in a similar way, you shall give to it **Fire**, until it becomes red as blood. Then truly you shall feed it further with **Milk** and suitable food until it grows to a mature age; for then it will be very strong and powerful to convert all lawful bodies into its own power and dignity. And this is the composition of our **Stone**, just as I have told you the truth in words. For indeed, to speak the truth without fiction, nothing else is to be sought but body from body, and light from light, where nevertheless fools go astray, seeking things that are only appearances and contrary to nature. For they endeavor in vain to dig out metals, from which no mortal has ever extracted [the Stone]. For from all things, nothing else is to be chosen in general but the 4 elements: **Sun**, **Moon**, **Fire**, **Earth**, and **Water**, which finally are all things, about which many speak much and fools babble in vain. For our **Sun** and **Moon** are not those from which the vessels of nobles and the wealthy are commonly made, but they are the seed extracted from a certain body in which all things are contained.

-> **Sun**, **Moon**, **Fire**. <-
